{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01RTZTLVgkXGPMu33gGVyqc3","type":"tool_result","content":"40\tfrom .tool_registry import GovernanceError, append_tools_governance, ensure_tools_dir, utc_now\n41\t\n42\t\n43\tdef is_judge_role(role: str | None) -> bool:\n44\t    return role in JUDGE_ROLES\n45\t\n46\t\n47\tdef is_supporting_role(role: str | None) -> bool:\n48\t    return role in SUPPORTING_ROLES\n49\t\n50\t\n51\tdef _coerce_severity(value: Any, *, fallback: str = \"medium\") -> str:\n52\t    \"\"\"feedback_store accepts {low, medium, high, critical}; agent envelope\n53\t    may use uppercase per finding schema. Normalize without rejecting.\"\"\"\n54\t    if not isinstance(value, str):\n55\t        return fallback\n56\t    normalized = value.strip().lower()\n57\t    if normalized in FEEDBACK_SEVERITIES:\n58\t        return normalized\n59\t    if normalized == \"informational\":\n60\t        return \"low\"\n61\t    return fallback\n62\t\n63\t\n64\tdef _verdict_field(details: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:\n65\t    \"\"\"The judge .md prompts shape verdicts under details.verdict (evidence,\n66\t    adversarial) or details.consensus (arbiter). Return whichever exists.\n67\t    \"\"\"\n68\t    if isinstance(details.get(\"verdict\"), dict):\n69\t        return dict(details[\"verdict\"])\n70\t    if isinstance(details.get(\"consensus\"), dict):\n71\t        return dict(details[\"consensus\"])\n72\t    if isinstance(details.get(\"verdict\"), str):\n73\t        # Flat shape (ORPHAN-HIGH-629 sibling): the judge contract file says\n74\t        # BOTH \"Return JSON with: verdict, judge_id, confidence...\" and\n75\t        # \"details.verdict.rationale\". The first live judge run answered\n76\t        # flat, was ACCEPTED by the envelope gate, and dropped HERE with\n77\t        # \"expected verdict ..., got None\" — a verdict lost between two\n78\t        # halves of one document. Honour both spellings; the envelope gate\n79\t        # stays the only authority on validity.\n80\t        return {\n81\t            key: details.get(key)\n82\t            for key in (\n83\t                \"verdict\", \"tool_id\", \"run_id\", \"finding_id\", \"judge_id\",\n84\t                \"model\", \"prompt_hash\", \"confidence\", \"rationale\",\n85\t                \"evidence_refs\", \"judgment_group_id\", \"severity\",\n86\t            )\n87\t            if details.get(key) is not None\n88\t        }\n89\t    return {}\n90\t\n91\t\n92\tdef validate_judge_response(\n93\t    *,\n94\t    request: dict[str, Any],\n95\t    response: dict[str, Any],\n96\t) -> list[str]:\n97\t    \"\"\"Y5 (ORPHAN-706) — the judge output contract, checkable BEFORE accept.\n98\t\n99\t    The second sealed night accepted 12 judge results whose envelopes never\n100\t    carried a verdict the bridge could read (\"expected verdict ..., got\n101\t    None\") — each one an accepted row that could never fold, burning bridge\n102\t    retries toward permanent_fail. This is the SINGLE definition of what a\n103\t    judge response must carry; ``record_judge_verdict_from_response`` raises\n104\t    on exactly this list, and the executor's pre-submit gate releases the\n105\t    claim on it instead of sealing an unfoldable result.\n106\t\n107\t    Returns [] for non-judge roles: the contract binds judges only.\n108\t    \"\"\"\n109\t    role = response.get(\"role\")\n110\t    if not is_judge_role(role):\n111\t        return []\n112\t    errors: list[str] = []\n113\t    details = response.get(\"details\") or {}\n114\t    if not isinstance(details, dict):\n115\t        details = {}\n116\t    verdict_block = _verdict_field(details)\n117\t    if not verdict_block:\n118\t        return [\"judge_verdict:absent\"]\n119\t    verdict = verdict_block.get(\"verdict\")\n120\t    if verdict not in FEEDBACK_VERDICTS:\n121\t        # ORPHAN-CRITICAL-735 — the arbiter's contract says \"return\n122\t        # details.consensus, OR the uncertainty reason when the consensus\n123\t        # gate cannot be met\", and the deterministic engine it mirrors\n124\t        # emits exactly that non-verdict outcome. The live arbiter obeyed\n125\t        # and this contract burned the claim as invalid:None — fourth\n126\t        # mint-vs-law instance. Uncertainty is a VALID arbitration\n127\t        # outcome, for the arbiter alone, with a reason from the closed\n128\t        # vocabulary; binary judges stay bound to binary verdicts.\n129\t        reason = verdict_block.get(\"uncertainty_reason\")\n130\t        if (\n131\t            role == \"consensus_arbitration\"\n132\t            and verdict is None\n133\t            and reason in CONSENSUS_UNCERTAINTY_REASONS\n134\t        ):\n135\t            pass\n136\t        else:\n137\t            errors.append(f\"judge_verdict.verdict:invalid:{verdict!r}\")\n138\t    tool_id = request.get(\"tool_id\") or verdict_block.get(\"tool_id\")\n139\t    run_id = request.get(\"run_id\") or verdict_block.get(\"run_id\")\n140\t    finding_id = request.get(\"finding_id\") or verdict_block.get(\"finding_id\")\n141\t    if not (tool_id and run_id and finding_id):\n142\t        errors.append(\"judge_verdict:missing_tool_run_or_finding_id\")\n143\t    judge_id = details.get(\"agent_subagent_type\") or verdict_block.get(\"judge_id\")\n144\t    if not judge_id:\n145\t        errors.append(\"judge_verdict:missing_judge_identity\")\n146\t    elif str(judge_id).startswith(\"ci-executor:\"):\n147\t        errors.append(\"judge_verdict:executor_shaped_judge_identity\")\n148\t    return errors\n149\t\n150\t\n151\tdef record_judge_verdict_from_response(\n152\t    *,\n153\t    request: dict[str, Any],\n154\t    response: dict[str, Any],\n155\t    base_dir: str | Path | None = None,\n156\t) -> dict[str, Any] | None:\n157\t    \"\"\"Translate an ACCEPTED judge response into an ai_judge feedback row.\n158\t\n159\t    Returns the persisted row, or None when the envelope role is not a\n160\t    judge role (the bridge is a no-op for non-judge submissions).\n161\t\n162\t    Raises GovernanceError when the response is missing the data the\n163\t    consensus engine requires (tool_id / run_id / finding_id come from\n164\t    the request envelope's optional fields; verdict + severity from\n165\t    the response.details).\n166\t    \"\"\"\n167\t    role = response.get(\"role\")\n168\t    if not is_judge_role(role):\n169\t        return None\n170\t\n171\t    # Y5 (ORPHAN-706) — one contract, two consumers: the executor's\n172\t    # pre-submit gate checks exactly this list and releases instead of\n173\t    # sealing an unfoldable accepted result; the bridge raises on it so a\n174\t    # row that somehow slipped past still fails loudly, never half-folds.\n175\t    contract_errors = validate_judge_response(request=request, response=response)\n176\t    if contract_errors:\n177\t        raise GovernanceError(\n178\t            \"judge bridge contract violation: \" + \"; \".join(contract_errors)\n179\t        )\n180\t\n181\t    details = response.get(\"details\") or {}\n182\t    if not isinstance(details, dict):\n183\t        details = {}\n184\t\n185\t    # ORPHAN-CRITICAL-735 — the arbiter's legitimate non-verdict outcome\n186\t    # folds through the SAME producer, ledger and idempotent escalation_id\n187\t    # as the deterministic engine, so one sweep drains both lanes into one\n188\t    # HUMAN_REQUIRED record. It is NOT a feedback verdict: an uncertain\n189\t    # arbitration must never write an ai_judge row.\n190\t    if role == \"consensus_arbitration\":\n191\t        arb_block = _verdict_field(details)\n192\t        arb_reason = arb_block.get(\"uncertainty_reason\")\n193\t        if arb_block.get(\"verdict\") is None and arb_reason in CONSENSUS_UNCERTAINTY_REASONS:\n194\t            from .feedback_store import record_consensus_uncertainty\n195\t\n196\t            row = record_consensus_uncertainty(\n197\t                tool_id=str(request.get(\"tool_id\") or arb_block.get(\"tool_id\") or \"\"),\n198\t                run_id=str(request.get(\"run_id\") or arb_block.get(\"run_id\") or \"\"),\n199\t                finding_id=str(request.get(\"finding_id\") or arb_block.get(\"finding_id\") or \"\"),\n200\t                group_id=str(request.get(\"judgment_group_id\") or arb_block.get(\"group_id\") or \"\"),\n201\t                reason=str(arb_reason),\n202\t                cycle_id=request.get(\"cycle_id\"),\n203\t                base_dir=base_dir,\n204\t            )\n205\t            return {\"kind\": \"consensus_uncertainty\", **row}\n206\t    verdict_block = _verdict_field(details)\n207\t\n208\t    tool_id = request.get(\"tool_id\") or verdict_block.get(\"tool_id\")\n209\t    run_id = request.get(\"run_id\") or verdict_block.get(\"run_id\")\n210\t    finding_id = request.get(\"finding_id\") or verdict_block.get(\"finding_id\")\n211\t    if not (tool_id and run_id and finding_id):\n212\t        raise GovernanceError(\n213\t            \"judge bridge requires tool_id, run_id, finding_id in request or response.details.verdict; \"\n214\t            f\"got tool_id={tool_id!r}, run_id={run_id!r}, finding_id={finding_id!r}\"\n215\t        )\n216\t\n217\t    verdict = verdict_block.get(\"verdict\")\n218\t    if verdict not in FEEDBACK_VERDICTS:\n219\t        raise GovernanceError(\n220\t            f\"judge bridge expected verdict in {FEEDBACK_VERDICTS}, got {verdict!r}\"\n221\t        )\n222\t    severity = _coerce_severity(verdict_block.get(\"severity\") or details.get(\"severity\"))\n223\t    # D1 (Kapalı Döngü) — judge identity. `response[\"agent_id\"]` is the\n224\t    # EXECUTOR's identity (`ci-executor:gha-<run>`), not the judge's: reading\n225\t    # it collapsed every judge drained by one workflow run into a single\n226\t    # voter (consensus structurally impossible) while judges drained by\n227\t    # DIFFERENT runs counted as distinct voters with meaningless identity.\n228\t    # The judge's real identity is the subagent the executor invoked\n229\t    # (`details.agent_subagent_type`, force-stamped by ci_executor); the"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"1e3ae1b1-9b85-4c9f-90ed-5bd54ae028a9","uuid":"d8f5e2cd-2a51-4d1d-bc2a-0318a7a60ac1","timestamp":"2026-09-19T23:24:55.516Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-evidence-judge-0823b429a9e6/aria-kernel/aria_kernel/judgment_bridge.py","content":"from .tool_registry import GovernanceError, append_tools_governance, ensure_tools_dir, utc_now\n\n\ndef is_judge_role(role: str | None) -> bool:\n    return role in JUDGE_ROLES\n\n\ndef is_supporting_role(role: str | None) -> bool:\n    return role in SUPPORTING_ROLES\n\n\ndef _coerce_severity(value: Any, *, fallback: str = \"medium\") -> str:\n    \"\"\"feedback_store accepts {low, medium, high, critical}; agent envelope\n    may use uppercase per finding schema. Normalize without rejecting.\"\"\"\n    if not isinstance(value, str):\n        return fallback\n    normalized = value.strip().lower()\n    if normalized in FEEDBACK_SEVERITIES:\n        return normalized\n    if normalized == \"informational\":\n        return \"low\"\n    return fallback\n\n\ndef _verdict_field(details: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"The judge .md prompts shape verdicts under details.verdict (evidence,\n    adversarial) or details.consensus (arbiter). Return whichever exists.\n    \"\"\"\n    if isinstance(details.get(\"verdict\"), dict):\n        return dict(details[\"verdict\"])\n    if isinstance(details.get(\"consensus\"), dict):\n        return dict(details[\"consensus\"])\n    if isinstance(details.get(\"verdict\"), str):\n        # Flat shape (ORPHAN-HIGH-629 sibling): the judge contract file says\n        # BOTH \"Return JSON with: verdict, judge_id, confidence...\" and\n        # \"details.verdict.rationale\". The first live judge run answered\n        # flat, was ACCEPTED by the envelope gate, and dropped HERE with\n        # \"expected verdict ..., got None\" — a verdict lost between two\n        # halves of one document. Honour both spellings; the envelope gate\n        # stays the only authority on validity.\n        return {\n            key: details.get(key)\n            for key in (\n                \"verdict\", \"tool_id\", \"run_id\", \"finding_id\", \"judge_id\",\n                \"model\", \"prompt_hash\", \"confidence\", \"rationale\",\n                \"evidence_refs\", \"judgment_group_id\", \"severity\",\n            )\n            if details.get(key) is not None\n        }\n    return {}\n\n\ndef validate_judge_response(\n    *,\n    request: dict[str, Any],\n    response: dict[str, Any],\n) -> list[str]:\n    \"\"\"Y5 (ORPHAN-706) — the judge output contract, checkable BEFORE accept.\n\n    The second sealed night accepted 12 judge results whose envelopes never\n    carried a verdict the bridge could read (\"expected verdict ..., got\n    None\") — each one an accepted row that could never fold, burning bridge\n    retries toward permanent_fail. This is the SINGLE definition of what a\n    judge response must carry; ``record_judge_verdict_from_response`` raises\n    on exactly this list, and the executor's pre-submit gate releases the\n    claim on it instead of sealing an unfoldable result.\n\n    Returns [] for non-judge roles: the contract binds judges only.\n    \"\"\"\n    role = response.get(\"role\")\n    if not is_judge_role(role):\n        return []\n    errors: list[str] = []\n    details = response.get(\"details\") or {}\n    if not isinstance(details, dict):\n        details = {}\n    verdict_block = _verdict_field(details)\n    if not verdict_block:\n        return [\"judge_verdict:absent\"]\n    verdict = verdict_block.get(\"verdict\")\n    if verdict not in FEEDBACK_VERDICTS:\n        # ORPHAN-CRITICAL-735 — the arbiter's contract says \"return\n        # details.consensus, OR the uncertainty reason when the consensus\n        # gate cannot be met\", and the deterministic engine it mirrors\n        # emits exactly that non-verdict outcome. The live arbiter obeyed\n        # and this contract burned the claim as invalid:None — fourth\n        # mint-vs-law instance. Uncertainty is a VALID arbitration\n        # outcome, for the arbiter alone, with a reason from the closed\n        # vocabulary; binary judges stay bound to binary verdicts.\n        reason = verdict_block.get(\"uncertainty_reason\")\n        if (\n            role == \"consensus_arbitration\"\n            and verdict is None\n            and reason in CONSENSUS_UNCERTAINTY_REASONS\n        ):\n            pass\n        else:\n            errors.append(f\"judge_verdict.verdict:invalid:{verdict!r}\")\n    tool_id = request.get(\"tool_id\") or verdict_block.get(\"tool_id\")\n    run_id = request.get(\"run_id\") or verdict_block.get(\"run_id\")\n    finding_id = request.get(\"finding_id\") or verdict_block.get(\"finding_id\")\n    if not (tool_id and run_id and finding_id):\n        errors.append(\"judge_verdict:missing_tool_run_or_finding_id\")\n    judge_id = details.get(\"agent_subagent_type\") or verdict_block.get(\"judge_id\")\n    if not judge_id:\n        errors.append(\"judge_verdict:missing_judge_identity\")\n    elif str(judge_id).startswith(\"ci-executor:\"):\n        errors.append(\"judge_verdict:executor_shaped_judge_identity\")\n    return errors\n\n\ndef record_judge_verdict_from_response(\n    *,\n    request: dict[str, Any],\n    response: dict[str, Any],\n    base_dir: str | Path | None = None,\n) -> dict[str, Any] | None:\n    \"\"\"Translate an ACCEPTED judge response into an ai_judge feedback row.\n\n    Returns the persisted row, or None when the envelope role is not a\n    judge role (the bridge is a no-op for non-judge submissions).\n\n    Raises GovernanceError when the response is missing the data the\n    consensus engine requires (tool_id / run_id / finding_id come from\n    the request envelope's optional fields; verdict + severity from\n    the response.details).\n    \"\"\"\n    role = response.get(\"role\")\n    if not is_judge_role(role):\n        return None\n\n    # Y5 (ORPHAN-706) — one contract, two consumers: the executor's\n    # pre-submit gate checks exactly this list and releases instead of\n    # sealing an unfoldable accepted result; the bridge raises on it so a\n    # row that somehow slipped past still fails loudly, never half-folds.\n    contract_errors = validate_judge_response(request=request, response=response)\n    if contract_errors:\n        raise GovernanceError(\n            \"judge bridge contract violation: \" + \"; \".join(contract_errors)\n        )\n\n    details = response.get(\"details\") or {}\n    if not isinstance(details, dict):\n        details = {}\n\n    # ORPHAN-CRITICAL-735 — the arbiter's legitimate non-verdict outcome\n    # folds through the SAME producer, ledger and idempotent escalation_id\n    # as the deterministic engine, so one sweep drains both lanes into one\n    # HUMAN_REQUIRED record. It is NOT a feedback verdict: an uncertain\n    # arbitration must never write an ai_judge row.\n    if role == \"consensus_arbitration\":\n        arb_block = _verdict_field(details)\n        arb_reason = arb_block.get(\"uncertainty_reason\")\n        if arb_block.get(\"verdict\") is None and arb_reason in CONSENSUS_UNCERTAINTY_REASONS:\n            from .feedback_store import record_consensus_uncertainty\n\n            row = record_consensus_uncertainty(\n                tool_id=str(request.get(\"tool_id\") or arb_block.get(\"tool_id\") or \"\"),\n                run_id=str(request.get(\"run_id\") or arb_block.get(\"run_id\") or \"\"),\n                finding_id=str(request.get(\"finding_id\") or arb_block.get(\"finding_id\") or \"\"),\n                group_id=str(request.get(\"judgment_group_id\") or arb_block.get(\"group_id\") or \"\"),\n                reason=str(arb_reason),\n                cycle_id=request.get(\"cycle_id\"),\n                base_dir=base_dir,\n            )\n            return {\"kind\": \"consensus_uncertainty\", **row}\n    verdict_block = _verdict_field(details)\n\n    tool_id = request.get(\"tool_id\") or verdict_block.get(\"tool_id\")\n    run_id = request.get(\"run_id\") or verdict_block.get(\"run_id\")\n    finding_id = request.get(\"finding_id\") or verdict_block.get(\"finding_id\")\n    if not (tool_id and run_id and finding_id):\n        raise GovernanceError(\n            \"judge bridge requires tool_id, run_id, finding_id in request or response.details.verdict; \"\n            f\"got tool_id={tool_id!r}, run_id={run_id!r}, finding_id={finding_id!r}\"\n        )\n\n    verdict = verdict_block.get(\"verdict\")\n    if verdict not in FEEDBACK_VERDICTS:\n        raise GovernanceError(\n            f\"judge bridge expected verdict in {FEEDBACK_VERDICTS}, got {verdict!r}\"\n        )\n    severity = _coerce_severity(verdict_block.get(\"severity\") or details.get(\"severity\"))\n    # D1 (Kapalı Döngü) — judge identity. `response[\"agent_id\"]` is the\n    # EXECUTOR's identity (`ci-executor:gha-<run>`), not the judge's: reading\n    # it collapsed every judge drained by one workflow run into a single\n    # voter (consensus structurally impossible) while judges drained by\n    # DIFFERENT runs counted as distinct voters with meaningless identity.\n    # The judge's real identity is the subagent the executor invoked\n    # (`details.agent_subagent_type`, force-stamped by ci_executor);    root = ensure_tools_dir(base_dir)\n    items = sample.get(\"items\") or []\n    existing = _existing_judge_dispatches(root)\n    # Y2 (ORPHAN-704) — backlog gate: when the executor's drain is behind,\n    # minting more envelopes only manufactures anchor-stale corpses. The\n    # ceiling is per role; freshly minted envelopes count toward it within\n    # this call so one large sample cannot blow through the cap.\n    pending = pending_judge_counts(base_dir=root) if max_pending_per_role is not None else {}\n    judged: dict[str, set[tuple[str, str]]] = {}\n    minted: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n    skipped: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n    for item in items:\n        if not isinstance(item, dict):\n            continue\n        group = _group_id(item)\n        prompt = _render_prompt(item)\n        refs = _evidence_refs(item)\n        tool_id = str(item.get(\"tool_id\") or \"\")\n        if tool_id not in judged:\n            judged[tool_id] = _judged_pairs(root, tool_id)\n        finding_key = _finding_key(item)\n        for role, agent in JUDGE_FANOUT:\n            if (group, agent) in existing:\n                skipped.append({\"judgment_group_id\": group, \"target_agent\": agent, \"reason\": \"already_dispatched\"})\n                continue\n            if (finding_key, agent) in judged[tool_id]:\n                skipped.append({\"judgment_group_id\": group, \"target_agent\": agent, \"reason\": \"already_judged\"})\n                continue\n            if max_pending_per_role is not None and pending.get(role, 0) >= max_pending_per_role:\n                skipped.append({\n                    \"judgment_group_id\": group, \"target_agent\": agent,\n                    \"reason\": \"mint_skipped_backlog\",\n                    \"pending\": pending.get(role, 0),\n                    \"max_pending_per_role\": max_pending_per_role,\n                })\n                continue\n            req = create_agent_invocation_request(\n                target_agent=agent,\n                role=role,\n                suggested_prompt=prompt,\n                must_satisfy=[{\"id\": \"verdict\", \"description\": \"Return true_positive or false_positive with file:line evidence\"}],\n                allowed_scope=[\"**\"],\n                evidence_refs=refs or None,\n                finding_id=str(item.get(\"finding_id\") or \"\"),\n                # ORPHAN-HIGH-765 — thread the sampler's fingerprint onto the\n                # mint so the verdict bridge can source identity from the\n                # request (D1) instead of trusting the judge to echo it.\n                finding_fingerprint=str(item.get(\"finding_fingerprint\") or \"\"),\n                tool_id=str(item.get(\"tool_id\") or \"\"),\n                run_id=str(item.get(\"run_id\") or \"\"),\n                judgment_group_id=group,\n                cycle_id=sample.get(\"cycle_id\"),\n                target_sha=target_sha,\n                base_dir=root,\n                context_repo_root=repo_root,\n            )\n            minted.append({\n                \"request_id\": req.get(\"request_id\"),\n                \"role\": role,\n                \"target_agent\": agent,\n                \"judgment_group_id\": group,\n            })\n            existing.add((group, agent))\n            if max_pending_per_role is not None:\n                pending[role] = pending.get(role, 0) + 1\n    return {\"schema_version\": 1, \"minted_count\": len(minted), \"minted\": minted, \"skipped\": skipped}\n\n\ndef _judge_rows_by_group(\n    tool_id: str,","numLines":70,"startLine":196,"totalLines":661}}}

conditions and only the first holds in substance. (1) Hazard: test-gap-adapter.ts:274-275 marks a file under /migrations/ hazardous when its text matches DROP|DELETE|TRUNCATE|ALTER TYPE. In this Baseline the up() at lines 6-32 is CREATE TYPE/TABLE/INDEX only; every DROP is in the TypeORM-generated down() at lines 34-60 (DROP TABLE event_streams:38, stored_events:54, projection_checkpoints:58, DROP TYPE:59), the mechanical inverse of up(). (2) Coverage: matchingTests at lines 296-320 accepts only a same-basename spec, a sibling or __tests__ spec, or a test whose static import declaration resolves to the file. No such spec exists for this Baseline, so the literal message is accurate under the adapter's static import graph. The substantive claim - that this hazardous migration runs with nothing exercising it - is contradicted at the workspace SHA. apps/db-migrate/src/__tests__/bootstrap-from-scratch.spec.ts lists event-store-service with migrationsDir apps/event-store-service/src/migrations (lines 290-295); loadMigrationClassesFromDir (448-497) requires every <13-digit>-<PascalName>.ts in that directory through createRequire (filename regex at 469, load at 482), and ds.runMigrations({transaction:'each'}) at line 922 executes the class against a fresh timescale/timescaledb-ha:pg16 container. Three assertion groups then fail if this migration does not apply cleanly: the event_store.migrations ledger row count must equal the on-disk file count (1243-1269), every event-store @Entity - stored_events, snapshots, event_streams, projection_checkpoints, exactly the tables this file creates - must exist with matching columns, FK and index counts (1283-1325), and SchemaDriftValidator must pass with SCHEMA_DRIFT_FATAL=true (1345-1414). That spec is a per-PR gate: .github/workflows/db-migration-check.yml:273-316 runs `npm run test:bootstrap`, which package.json:125 binds to this spec. The adapter cannot see any of this because the coverage is directory-manifest plus dynamic require, and in this service nothing statically imports the migration at all by design: app.module.ts:62 and database/data-source.ts:34 register migrations by glob, and the comment at app.module.ts:32-36 records that the glob replaced an explicit array which had silently missed two migrations. A heuristic whose only importing-test signal is a static import declaration flags every glob-wired migration in this repository regardless of real coverage; that is a structural blind spot of the rule, not a gap in the repository. The residue the finding could still point at is down(): nothing executes this Baseline's down(), and a rollback path exists (apps/db-migrate/src/migration-orchestrator.ts:647 calls executor.undoLastMigration(), exercised in rollback.integration.spec.ts with a stand-alone marker class at lines 34-46). That residue is why confidence is 0.82 rather than higher. It does not carry the finding: a baseline down() is TypeORM's generated inverse, it is reachable only after every later event-store migration (six of them at this SHA) has been reverted, and a test of it would assert that the event ledger tables disappear - confirming destruction, not guarding against it. Correct path, tier 2 (make it automatic) in the adapter: treat a test that loads a migrations directory through a manifest or glob - the bootstrap spec's SERVICES[].migrationsDir - as a coverage signal for every timestamped file in that directory, so glob-wired services stop producing this finding class; a per-Baseline allowlist entry would be the weaker tier-3 substitute. No `as any`, skip, or suppression is involved in either path.\",\n      \"evidence_refs\": [\n        \"apps/event-store-service/src/migrations/1800000000000-Baseline.ts:6\",\n
